# AEGIS PMA — CIO Brief · Tuesday 2026-10-27

**Session:** US session Tue 27 Oct 2026, opens 21:30 SGT (US on daylight time until 1 Nov) · **AQE data:** to the Mon 26 Oct close (export dated 27 Oct 08:51 SGT).

**Run state:** committee closed · 50 names voted (35 nominated + 2 held + 3 reclaim seats + 10 strength seats) · **9 ADVANCE · 29 HOLD-FOR-CONDITIONS · 12 PASS** · ranking purity PASS · crowding audit clean (gap -33 points, no warning).

**Bottom line:** Nine names advance: eight still need a print above their buy level and VSXY is already above its first one, with SPY 0.7% above its 760 put wall and Valen at RISK OFF. The book is MP and V only; nothing was entered on Monday, so today's room is judged fresh.

Six of the nine are new advances today (IOT, DBX, VSXY, HOG, TRV, TEAM), ESTC and CNQ repeat at about the same entry, and CVE returns after 20 Oct. Dealers are short gamma 1.2% under the flip, and FOMC lands Wed 28 Oct at 14:00 ET (02:00 SGT Thu).

The 24 declared items sit in the table directly under the verdict table.

## §0 — VERDICT TABLE

**How to read the tables.** ⛔ marks a name on Valen's no-buy list; all 38 ADVANCE and HOLD names carry it because the one shared flag is the market check (RISK OFF), not a name-by-name judgment.

The fundamentals consult has no vote: its grade reads agrees / neutral / contradicts. Valen setup is the best house-setup status on the AQE row (— = none); U&R is the AQE reclaim column (MET = reclaim day, SETUP = forming).

Conditions read as buy above X, too late above Z, exit on a daily close under Y.

**Extra Valen facts beyond the shared flag (plain facts, never a reason to skip a name):** CTAS 4.7 ATR above its 20-day average; SLDE 4.1 ATR above its 20-day average; CART 4.8 ATR above its 20-day average; BOX 4.9 ATR above its 20-day average.

### Declared today (24 items; read these with the verdict table)

| # | Item | What was declared | What it means for you |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Run storage | The run sat on local disposable disk until PUBLISH (no mid-run checkpoint pushes, by design: git_sync maps outside aegis/output/pma/). | If the container is lost before PUBLISH, the run has to be re-done; nothing is half-saved. |
| 2 | Seats and the fundamentals consult | Seats ran as general-purpose agents reading their card and packet by path. The fundamentals analyst is consulted only, no vote (R22, 10 voters). | Same method for every voice. The fundamentals read appears per name as information and changed no verdict. |
| 3 | Fundamentals | Fundamentals pack 98 of 98 names: 81 carried from the 26 Oct pack, 17 pulled fresh on 27 Oct (IOT WELL CTAS VSXY TXN CRWD UGI UNP A LUV RTX PATH APLE ERIC CVE TEAM ETSY). CNQ growth is reported in CAD; ratios are unitless. | Every name in the room has a fundamentals read; 81 of them are one day old. FMP's quote tool is not on the plan, so no batch quote was used anywhere in this run. |
| 4 | Challenge forms | The challenge forms are not covered by the figure audit. The conductor traced every figure by hand: 91/91, 57/57 and 66/66 on the three challenge desks, and 317/320 on the fundamentals consult within printed rounding; the 3 others are the consult's derived proxy-PEG figures (UNP 2.8, PATH 1.7, FAST 4.2), labelled derived arithmetic. | A compensating check, not an audit-grade proof. Desk names are in Appendix A. |
| 5 | Vote packet | The vote packet is 135 KB, over the soft limit; not trimmed. | Declared, left whole. |
| 6 | Scorecard closes | Scorecard closes were pulled from FMP one symbol at a time (the batch quote is not on the plan). ACA has no 26 Oct close (FMP returned empty twice). | ACA rows grade as missing for the 21, 22 and 23 Oct runs; every other row is graded on a pulled close. |
| 7 | Scratchpad collision | The seats share one scratchpad directory; one nominating seat's helper build.py was overwritten by another seat's script and its next run executed it unread. It printed the other seat's nomination tickers (CRWD, UGI, GTLB, SLDE, ERIC, CVE) and the held names MP, V. The seat says its nominations were fixed before that run and changed nothing after; its nominations (IOT, DBX, HOG, SAIL, TRV, GLBE, MTCH, BBY, VSXY) have zero overlap with the leaked set. | Declared as a minor blindness breach. The conductor checked that both seats' form hashes equal the validated hashes (13b8022e..., dac18c68...), so neither file was rewritten; the form was kept, not respawned (a respawn costs about 245k tokens and the leak touched no pick). Remaining seats were told to use seat-named scratch files. Seat names are in Appendix A. |
| 8 | Druckenmiller packet | Built from Crown's data_points (name, value) and key_levels (market, level, distance_pct) only; Crown's reading and meaning prose fields were dropped, per RUN.yaml MACRO must_also. The 26 Oct packet carried them; this one is stricter. | His read is not like-for-like with yesterday's: he had no Crown prose to anchor on. |
| 9 | Second scratch-name case | One more nominating seat also used generic scratch names (an.py, an2.py, an3.py, build.py) before the conductor notice. It reports no cross-seat read, re-verified every figure in its form against its own packet with a seat-named script (0 mismatches), and the form hash is unchanged (3d16ea88...). | Nothing further to declare. Seat name is in Appendix A. |
| 10 | Conditions re-run | CONDITIONS RE-RUN: the first compile used --forms 'forms/r2/*.json' (RUN.yaml text); pma_conditions skips .partNofM files and merged ballots live in forms/r2merged/, so it found no ballots (empty conditions.md, every name "not structured"). Caught by a trial emit before CHECK; re-run with --forms 'forms/r2merged/*.json'. The ticked CONDITIONS step was redone, the WRITE agent had already started, so it was told by message to discard the empty files and re-read the regenerated ones before writing; the delivered brief is checked against the new files at CHECK. | This brief reads the regenerated conditions files only. |
| 11 | Rank arguments | RANK ran --cap 24 --door-budget 10 (RULINGS R26 says 'now 10'; RUN.yaml text still says 8; 26 Oct ran at 10). Room 50 = 35 nominated + 2 held + 3 R25 reclaim seats (OXY TEAM ETSY) + 10 R27 strength seats (BRZE DXC ACN NTSK CART TENB ASAN TTAN BOX VERX). | The door budget in force is 10. ## §0A — EXECUTIVE CONSENSUS: WHY THESE ADVANCE

**The book in three points.**

1. **What they share:** all nine sit within 5.0% of a 52-week high and are 6.7 to 25.2 points ahead of SPY over 20 days; eight still need a print above their buy level and VSXY is already above its first one. They are fewer bets than nine: four technology or software (IOT, DBX, ESTC, TEAM), two energy (CNQ, CVE), TRV in V's sector.
2. **Where agreement was strongest and thinnest:** IOT very strong agreement; DBX very strong agreement; VSXY very strong agreement; HOG narrow; ESTC carried, nothing said against it; TRV carried, nothing said against it; CNQ broad, over one objection; CVE broad, over one objection; TEAM broad, over one objection. No name reached conviction 4; all nine sit at 3.
3. **What invalidates the group:** SPY losing the 760 put wall (0.70% under the last close) with dealers short gamma, or a hostile FOMC print (Wed 28 Oct 14:00 ET, 02:00 SGT Thu); either removes the cushion under all nine at once.

### IOT — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · very strong agreement

The deciding voices read a Stage 2 leader just through its pivot: relative strength at the 91st percentile, momentum 83 accelerating and every long-term average in order under price. The strongest objection is that the break printed on thin volume (0.9x normal) and the fundamentals read contradicts it (fundamentals consult, no vote: sales growth slowing 0.437, 0.333, 0.296 and a P/E of 276); it lost because the plan buys only above 45.32 on at least 1.4x normal volume, so the thin print is not what is bought. What must stay true: a daily close above 43.235; the plan's window is narrow, 45.32 to the too-late price 45.5363.

### DBX — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · very strong agreement

Most of the room read a tight coil under 38.17 with relative strength at the 92nd percentile and 18.2 points ahead of SPY; the fundamentals read agrees (fundamentals consult, no vote: cash yield 11.6% at a P/E of 20.8, though sales are flat and equity is negative). The strongest objection is that no break has printed, volume is 0.9x normal and the force reading faded from four 10s to 7, so a dip may still be forming; it lost because the plan buys only above 38.17 on at least 1.4x volume, not the coil. What must stay true: a daily close above 37.61, which is 0.3% under the last close.

### VSXY — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · very strong agreement

The deciding voices backed the pivot clear at 93.39 with 1.3x normal volume, relative strength at the 95th percentile and five strong chart lenses; the last close is 1.7% above that level and 96.55 is the second buy level. The strongest objection is bearish divergence on two oscillators, a flow score of 39.5 and a fundamentals read that contradicts (fundamentals consult, no vote: debt-to-equity 2.93, sales +5.2%, EPS -2.4%); it lost because the break has the volume and the exit is simple, a daily close under 93.39. What must stay true: price holds above 93.39 and volume stays at 1.2x or better; the plan's too-late price is 98.0595.

### HOG — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · narrow

A narrow call: the deciding voices backed the clear of 28.86 (relative strength at the 81st percentile, 17.0 points ahead of SPY over 20 days, momentum 87 accelerating). The objections were that the break has no volume (0.88x normal), sales and earnings are falling (-13.8% and -18.5%), four of six chart lenses read blank and the fundamentals read contradicts (fundamentals consult, no vote: auto maker in a down-cycle, free-cash-flow yield -4.4%); it carried at conviction 3 on the pivot and the trend. What must stay true: a close above 28.86 arrives with volume, and a daily close under 28.375 ends it; the plan's too-late price is 29.7258.

### ESTC — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · carried, nothing said against it

Carried for strength: a new high with momentum 77 accelerating, a flow score of 86.8 and volume 1.38x normal. The objection from the non-deciding voices was the 19 Nov report; it lost because the plan buys only above 107.09 on at least 1.4x volume with an exit on a daily close under 101.66, and a gap against the position before the print is event risk for the PM to weigh. The fundamentals read is neutral (fundamentals consult, no vote: EPS flips sign yearly, so the P/E of 27 is not clean); what must stay true is no gap against it before 19 Nov, and the plan's too-late price is 110.303.

### TRV — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · carried, nothing said against it

Carried on leadership: momentum 84 and accelerating, relative strength at the 85th percentile, a shallow 14.2% base and the Insurance theme LEADING; the fundamentals read agrees (fundamentals consult, no vote: cash yield 12.6%, though float flatters the cash). The objection was thin volume (0.84x) and that TRV stacks with held V in Financials; it carried because the plan requires 1.2x volume before any buy. The plan's levels conflict: one analyst buys above 405.44 and another above 402.78 while the plan's own too-late price is 403.19, so the usable window is 402.78 to 403.19, only 0.1% wide; the exit is a daily close under 377.15.

### CNQ — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · broad, over one objection

Carried over a real objection: relative strength at the 78th percentile, momentum 93 accelerating and earnings up 81% (fundamentals consult, no vote: neutral, a rebound after two down years whose 25% net margin argues caution near a peak). The objection was thin volume (0.68x) at the high, and that energy is one bet with CVE; it lost because the plan buys only above 55.29 on at least 1.2x volume, with the too-late price at 56.9487. What must stay true: a daily close above 51.00.

### CVE — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · broad, over one objection

Broad agreement on the reclaim and a lens block with coil, leadership, institutional money and resistance all strong; 6.7 points ahead of SPY over 20 days. The strongest objection is that the 20-day average (32.11) is still under the 50-day (32.23), volume is 0.62x and the momentum voices see a dip still running; it lost because the plan buys only above 33.32 and exits on a daily close under 32.32, with the too-late price at 33.9864. The fundamentals read is neutral (fundamentals consult, no vote: sales -14% with EPS +29%, P/E 12.9, cycle position not readable); it is the same energy bet as CNQ.

### TEAM — ADVANCE · conviction 3 · broad, over one objection

Reserved reclaim seat (reclaim level 221.09: it dipped below the rising trendline on the last session and closed back above it, so this is the reclaim day); it then carried the case on 25.2 points ahead of SPY over 20 days, a six-month gain of 222.8% and a strong chart-lens block, in a leading software theme. The strongest objection, from one deciding voice, was a climax reading after that run and light volume (0.74x); it lost because the plan's own limits are the too-late price 234.212 and volume of at least 1.2x, with the buy only above 227.39 and the exit on a daily close under 219.91. The fundamentals read is neutral (fundamentals consult, no vote: sales re-accelerating 0.197 then 0.26, GAAP breakeven, cash positive).

### Scorecard (grading only; no verdict changed)

As of the 26 Oct close, seven runs from 15 to 26 Oct. ADVANCE calls pooled (63): mean +0.90%, median +0.31%, 38 of 63 beat SPY, and they trail the equal-weight AQE list they were picked from by 0.20 points; on full 5-session windows only (25 calls) +2.21%, 21 of 25 beat SPY, still 0.33 points behind the list. Most windows are partial and the sample is small and overlapping, so this does not prove the ADVANCE label adds value over the list (PASS calls were 0.09 points ahead of it, HOLD calls 0.11 behind).

## §0B — WHAT THESE WERE PICKED ON, AND HOW THEY FIT A MOMENTUM BOOK

7 ADVANCE names (IOT, VSXY, HOG, ESTC, TRV, CNQ, TEAM) were decided in the MOMENTUM lane, where the strength voices decide; two (DBX, CVE) were decided in the PULLBACK lane by the whole room. MOMENTUM names are never marked down for sitting above a trigger: distance above a buy level and distance to the stop are plain facts, and the only limits are the plan's too-late price, its volume and beat-SPY conditions, and the market floor (the 760 put wall).

Seated by rule, not nominated: TEAM is an ADVANCE on a reserved reclaim seat (no voice nominated it); the strength seats (BRZE DXC ACN NTSK CART TENB ASAN TTAN BOX VERX) drew no ADVANCE. Challenge desks flagged that technology is one cluster rather than independent bets, and that several MOMENTUM names broke on thin volume; both are covered by the volume condition in each plan (Appendix A has the desks' words).

### Dealer gamma

| Index | Dealer regime | Total gamma | Valen light |
|---|---|---|---|
| SPY | NEGATIVE (dealers short gamma, moves amplified both ways) | -$8.49bn | RED |
| QQQ | NEGATIVE (dealers short gamma, moves amplified both ways) | -$4.29bn | not given (Valen light covers SPY only) |

Valen's dealer read, one word swapped to the plain term floor: "Volatile market — dealers are short gamma and amplify moves in both directions: floor ~760, breakout level ~775 (reclaiming it calms the market), resistance ~780."

*Caveat (one line):* Customers are long calls and long puts, so dealers are long call gamma and short put gamma. This is the standard convention, NOT observed data — exchange feeds publish open interest, never who is on which side.

*Read:* the 760 put wall is the market floor the live review checks; a close back above the 774.62 flip would put dealers back to damping moves.

Crowded-long Nasdaq and Russell money would reverse fast on a break | Crown |

|---|---|---|---|

Its sentence "collect premium against it" is contradicted by its own numbers: dealers are short gamma and VIX is above the 15 premium-selling threshold |

### A4 — Seat declarations and data gaps

#### detect-lens

- no round 1 form on file for this seat
- round 2 declaration: canon_status: HAND-TRANSCRIBED, UNGROUNDED (no lock, no sealed extract). lens_positive is an unweighted, untested reading aid, never a gate; votes are lens and MA-spine reads.
- round 2 declaration: tape_read NOT_APPLICABLE (premarket); spread_read NOT_APPLICABLE; gap_filter NOT_SERVED; ranking is the lens count, not Clenow's regression-slope ranking.
- round 2 declaration: trend_filter: ma_50/ma_100/ma_200 spine. exit_philosophy: fixed stop on a ma_50 loss, not rank-only.
- round 2 declaration: Rule: conviction scales with lens_positive, less one for a partial spine or two lens warns; ties go to runner_conviction 3 or more; four '--' lenses or a null lens block is ABSTAIN.
- round 2 declaration: Advisory only, not voted: sc_momentum, structure, structure_shift, energy, flow. Divergence and fundamentals are off my menu. VALEN setups cited as weights only (R14, R1).
- round 2 data gap (lens leadership/coil/insti_money/resistance on HOG ESTC TRV): four of six lenses '--': lens_positive not comparable; ABSTAIN, spine read only
- round 2 data gap (lens block on MP): null: no lens read; inverted MA spine read only
- round 2 data gap (lens leadership/coil/insti_money/resistance on ETSY BRZE): '--': ABSTAIN, lens_positive not comparable

#### crown (macro seat)

- limit: CONTESTED (C37): the artifact's family and so_what say own the average stock and collect premium, but gamma_positive is unmet and both premium-selling tests fail. Surfaced, not resolved.
- limit: Scenario score is a share of conditions met, not a probability, never fitted to history. This seat does not size, name a ticker or place anything; 1.00x is a multiplier on the PM's own risk, set from the CTA dial only.
- limit: Standalone by charter (10.6): the packet also carries SRM, Macro Weather, thematic basket, intermarket and AQE regime blocks; none were read. Cross-checking 'the rally is broad' against index direction belongs to the PM and other seats.
- limit: Freshness: vol leg 10-23 misses the 10-26 session; CFTC 10-20 is 7 days old; newest_source is dated 10-27 though generated before the US 10-27 open, so it is not a completed cash close (source not named in packet).
- limit: Artifact prose vs numbers: 'single stocks move about as much as the index' vs 41.9 vs 18.4; 'not crowded' (trend funds) beside 'fragile setup, a lot of money leaning the same way'; warnings_lit 10 vs '1 lit plus 9'.
- limit: Dealer map rests on the standard sign convention (customers long calls and puts), not observed data. Large-spec crowding is context, never timing.
- limit: what_changed is empty: packet cannot distinguish no material change from no prior run. Runner-up scenario score is absent, so contested=false cannot be checked.
